待核查：Machine extraction requires expert review; chemical accuracy is not verified.；Scheme 3 footnote calls the substrate 1; arrow/general procedure call it 4. Quantity is linked to the row-specific alpha-naphthol without silently correcting the source text.；Page-7 NMR, optical rotation, and HRMS were retained as characterization context and were not independently checked peak-by-peak.；The chiral HPLC retention peaks are labeled major/minor, but the page does not explicitly assign each peak to an absolute configuration.；The isomeric SMILES is a structure-derived machine candidate; standard stereo parsing does not establish chemical correctness.
